    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}
})();</script></div></div></div></div></div></nav><main id="content-container" class="lg:flex-1 lg:min-w-0"><script <h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="constraints"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#constraints" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Constraints</span></h3>
<ul>
<li><code>fromAmount</code> must be in smallest unit (e.g., 6 decimals for USDC)</li>
<li><code>slippage</code> is a decimal (0.005 = 0.5%, not 0.5)</li>
<li>Quote expires after ~60 seconds</li>
</ul>
<hr/>

<span data-as="p"><strong>Action</strong>: Check the status of a cross-chain transfer.</span>

<span data-as="p">After <code>/advanced/routes</code> to get transaction data for each step.</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="get-/connections"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#get-/connections" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">GET /connections</span></h2>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Action</strong>: Get all possible token connections between two chains.</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">      ]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  ]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>

node\n\n/**\n * Auto-translation script for LI.FI documentation.\n * Translates English documentation to Simplified Chinese (zh-Hans).\n *\n * Modes\n *   node scripts/translate-docs.js                     changed files vs the diff base\n *   node scripts/translate-docs.js \u003cfile\u003e [\u003cfile\u003e...]   explicit files\n *   node scripts/translate-docs.js --all                every mapped page (catch-up pass)\n *   node scripts/translate-docs.js --dry-run            plan only, no API call, no API key\n *\n * Flags\n *   --dry-run             print the plan and token estimates, write nothing\n *   --all                 ignore git, walk every mapped English page\n *   --base \u003cref\u003e          diff base for change detection (default: CI-provided)\n *   --include-optional    also translate the optional sections (cli, mcp-server, docs)\n *   --limit \u003cn\u003e           cap the number of files processed in this run\n *\n * Environment\n *   OPENAI_API_KEY                  required unless --dry-run\n *   TRANSLATION_MODEL               default gpt-4o\n *   TRANSLATION_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS   default 8000\n *   TRANSLATE_INCLUDE_OPTIONAL      true to enable the optional sections\n *   TRANSLATE_BASE_REF              diff base branch name (CI)\n *   TRANSLATE_BEFORE_SHA            previous sha for push events (CI)\n *\n * Failure policy: this script fails loudly. A file that cannot be translated\n * cleanly is never written, and any failure makes the process exit non-zero.\n */\n\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\nconst { execFileSync } = require('child_process');\n\nconst config = require('./translation-config');\nconst { splitMarkdown, checkStructuralParity, scanStructure } = require('./markdown-chunker');\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CLI parsing\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction parseArgs(argv) {\n  const opts = {\n    dryRun: false,\n    all: false,\n    includeOptional: config.includeOptionalFromEnv(argv),\n    base: process.env.TRANSLATE_BASE_REF || null,\n    limit: null,\n    files: []\n  };\n\n  for (let i = 0; i \u003c argv.length; i++) {\n    const arg = argv[i];\n    if (arg === '--dry-run') opts.dryRun = true;\n    else if (arg === '--all') opts.all = true;\n    else if (arg === '--include-optional') opts.includeOptional = true;\n    else if (arg === '--base') opts.base = argv[++i];\n    else if (arg === '--limit') {\n      const raw = argv[++i];\n      if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(String(raw)) || Number(raw) \u003c 1) {\n        console.error(`--limit expects a positive integer, received: ${raw}`);\n        process.exit(2);\n      }\n      opts.limit = Number(raw);\n    }\n    else if (arg.startsWith('--')) {\n      console.error(`Unknown flag: ${arg}`);\n      process.exit(2);\n    } else opts.files.push(config.toRepoRelative(arg));\n  }\n  return opts;\n}\n\n// Only consume argv when run as a CLI. When required as a module (the offline\n// self-test) the caller's own flags must not be parsed as translation flags.\nconst options =\n  require.main === module\n    ? parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2))\n    : { dryRun: false, all: false, includeOptional: false, base: null, limit: null, files: [] };\n\n// openai and gray-matter are only needed for a live run. Requiring them lazily\n// keeps --dry-run working on a fresh clone with no node_modules and no API key.\nlet openai = null;\nfunction getClient() {\n  if (!openai) {\n    const OpenAI = require('openai');\n    openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY });\n  }\n  return openai;\n}\n\nlet matter = null;\nfunction getMatter() {\n  if (!matter) matter = require('gray-matter');\n  return matter;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Change detection\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction git(args) {\n  return execFileSync('git', args, { encoding: 'utf-8', cwd: config.ROOT }).trim();\n}\n\nfunction refExists(ref) {\n  try {\n    git(['rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', `${ref}^{commit}`]);\n    return true;\n  } catch (error) {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve a base ref to the merge base it shares with HEAD.\n *\n * \"What this branch changed\" is the diff from the merge base, not from the tip of\n * the base branch and not from the previous commit. Computing the merge base\n * explicitly (rather than leaning on the `A...B` shorthand) means a shallow clone\n * with no common ancestor fails here with a message that names the cause.\n */\nfunction resolveMergeBase(ref) {\n  let mergeBase;\n  try {\n    mergeBase = git(['merge-base', ref, 'HEAD']);\n  } catch (error) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `No common ancestor between \"${ref}\" and HEAD. The checkout is probably shallow; ` +\n        'the workflow needs fetch-depth: 0.'\n    );\n  }\n  if (!mergeBase) throw new Error(`git merge-base returned nothing for \"${ref}\"`);\n  return mergeBase;\n}\n\n/**\n * Work out which two points to diff.\n *\n * Previously this was hard-coded to `HEAD~1 HEAD`, which on a pull request only\n * ever saw the newest commit on the branch. Everything else in the PR was\n * invisible and the run exited \"No files to translate\" in about 20 seconds.\n *\n * The `HEAD~1` fallback is now local-only and loud. In CI it was worse than the\n * bug it replaced: a manual `changed` run arrives with an empty `github.base_ref`,\n * fell through to `HEAD~1`, and reported the newest commit as \"what this branch\n * changed\". On a multi-commit branch that silently skips most of the work.\n */\nfunction resolveDiffRange() {\n  const eventName = process.env.GITHUB_EVENT_NAME || 'local';\n\n  if (options.base) {\n    const base = options.base;\n    for (const candidate of [base, `origin/${base}`]) {\n      if (refExists(candidate)) {\n        const mergeBase = resolveMergeBase(candidate);\n        return {\n          range: `${mergeBase}..HEAD`,\n          describe: `${mergeBase}..HEAD (merge base with ${candidate})`\n        };\n      }\n    }\n    try {\n      git(['fetch', '--no-tags', '--depth=0', 'origin', base]);\n    } catch (error) {\n      // fall through to the hard failure below\n    }\n    for (const candidate of [`origin/${base}`, 'FETCH_HEAD']) {\n      if (refExists(candidate)) {\n        const mergeBase = resolveMergeBase(candidate);\n        return {\n          range: `${mergeBase}..HEAD`,\n          describe: `${mergeBase}..HEAD (merge base with fetched ${candidate})`\n        };\n      }\n    }\n    throw new Error(`Cannot resolve diff base \"${base}\". Checkout needs fetch-depth: 0 and access to the base branch.`);\n  }\n\n  if (eventName === 'pull_request' || eventName === 'pull_request_target') {\n    throw new Error(\n      'Running on a pull_request event but no diff base was provided. ' +\n        'Set TRANSLATE_BASE_REF (github.base_ref) in the workflow.'\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (eventName === 'workflow_dispatch') {\n    throw new Error(\n      'Running mode=changed on a manual dispatch but no diff base was provided. ' +\n        'A manual run has no pull request, so there is nothing to infer a base from. ' +\n        'Set the base_ref workflow input (TRANSLATE_BASE_REF), or run mode=all.'\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (eventName === 'push') {\n    const before = process.env.TRANSLATE_BEFORE_SHA || '';\n    if (before \u0026\u0026 !/^0+$/.test(before) \u0026\u0026 refExists(before)) {\n      return { range: `${before}..HEAD`, describe: `${before}..HEAD (push event)` };\n    }\n    throw new Error(\n      'Running on a push event but TRANSLATE_BEFORE_SHA is missing or unusable. Pass --base \u003cref\u003e or --all.'\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Local runs only. Prefer the merge base with the default branch; fall back to\n  // the parent commit with a warning so a one-commit local check still works.\n  for (const candidate of ['origin/main', 'main']) {\n    if (refExists(candidate)) {\n      const mergeBase = resolveMergeBase(candidate);\n      return {\n        range: `${mergeBase}..HEAD`,\n        describe: `${mergeBase}..HEAD (local, merge base with ${candidate})`\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!refExists('HEAD~1')) {\n    throw new Error('No diff base available: HEAD has no parent. Pass --base \u003cref\u003e or --all.');\n  }\n  console.warn(\n    'WARNING: no base branch found, diffing HEAD~1..HEAD. This is the newest commit only, ' +\n      'not everything this branch changed. Pass --base \u003cref\u003e for a branch-wide diff.'\n  );\n  return { range: 'HEAD~1..HEAD', describe: 'HEAD~1..HEAD (local fallback, newest commit only)' };\n}\n\n/**\n * Return { changed: [paths], deleted: [paths] } for the resolved range.\n * Any git failure is fatal: a silent empty list is what hid this bug for months.\n */\nfunction findChangedFiles() {\n  const { range, describe } = resolveDiffRange();\n  console.log(`Diff range: ${describe}`);\n\n  let raw;\n  try {\n    raw = execFileSync('git', ['diff', '--name-status', '--find-renames', range], {\n      encoding: 'utf-8',\n      cwd: config.ROOT\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    throw new Error(`git diff failed for range ${range}: ${error.message}`);\n  }\n\n  const changed = [];\n  const deleted = [];\n  for (const line of raw.split('\\n').filter(Boolean)) {\n    const parts = line.split('\\t');\n    const status = parts[0];\n    if (status.startsWith('R')) {\n      deleted.push(parts[1]);\n      changed.push(parts[2]);\n    } else if (status.startsWith('D')) {\n      deleted.push(parts[1]);\n    } else {\n      changed.push(parts[1]);\n    }\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Changed files in range: ${changed.length} modified/added, ${deleted.length} deleted/renamed-from`);\n  return { changed, deleted };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Translation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst BODY_PROMPT_HEADER = `You are a professional technical translator specializing in blockchain and cryptocurrency documentation.\n\nTranslate the following technical documentation from English to Simplified Chinese (zh-Hans).\n\nCRITICAL RULES:\n1. Preserve ALL markdown and MDX formatting exactly (headers, lists, code blocks, JSX components, links, images).\n2. DO NOT translate:\n   - Code snippets and code blocks\n   - API endpoints and URLs\n   - Function names and variable names\n   - File paths\n   - JSX component names and their prop names\n   - Brand names (LI.FI, Ethereum, Solana, Bitcoin, etc.)\n   - Technical abbreviations (API, SDK, DEX, EVM, etc.)\n3. Keep technical terms in English when commonly used (for example bridge, swap, route, quote).\n4. Maintain exact structure and spacing.\n5. Preserve HTML comments and frontmatter delimiters.\n6. Keep all special characters and symbols.\n7. Translate the ENTIRE input. Never summarise, never omit a section, never stop early.`;\n\nfunction buildBodyPrompt(chunk, chunkIndex, chunkCount) {\n  const partNote =\n    chunkCount \u003e 1\n      ? `\\nThis is part ${chunkIndex + 1} of ${chunkCount} of one page. Translate this part only. Do not add an introduction, a conclusion, or any part markers.\\n`\n      : '\\n';\n\n  return `${BODY_PROMPT_HEADER}\n${partNote}\nEnglish Content:\n---\n${chunk}\n---\n\nReturn ONLY the translated markdown content with no explanations or additional text.\nDo NOT wrap the output in a code block (no \\`\\`\\`markdown or \\`\\`\\` at the start/end).`;\n}\n\nfunction stripCodeBlockWrapper(text) {\n  const match = text.match(/^```(?:markdown|md|mdx)?\\s*\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n```\\s*$/);\n  return match ? match[1].trim() : text;\n}\n\n/**\n * The prompt fences the source in `---` delimiters, and the model sometimes\n * echoes them back around its answer. Left in place, gray-matter reads the\n * page's opening paragraph as frontmatter and re-serialises it as a\n * character-indexed YAML map, which is how several committed Chinese pages lost\n * their lead-in section.\n *\n * Only strip when the source chunk did not itself open and close on a horizontal\n * rule, so a page whose own markdown uses `---` at both ends survives untouched.\n */\nfunction stripEchoedDelimiters(text, sourceChunk) {\n  const wrapped = /^---\\s*\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n---\\s*$/;\n  if (wrapped.test(sourceChunk)) return text;\n  const match = text.match(wrapped);\n  if (!match) return text;\n  console.log('  Stripped echoed --- content delimiters from the completion');\n  return match[1].trim();\n}\n\n/**\n * A numeric frontmatter key means prose was parsed as YAML somewhere upstream.\n * Refuse the page rather than committing the corruption.\n */\nfunction assertFrontmatterUsable(frontmatter, filename) {\n  const numericKey = Object.keys(frontmatter).find((key) =\u003e /^\\d+$/.test(key));\n  if (numericKey !== undefined) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `${filename}: frontmatter contains the numeric key \"${numericKey}\", which means a prose ` +\n        'string was parsed as YAML. Refusing to write a corrupted page.'\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Truncation guard. The old script read `choices[0].message.content` and wrote\n * it to disk regardless of why the model stopped, so a completion clipped at the\n * token ceiling became a committed half-page. Anything other than a clean stop\n * throws here and the file is never written.\n */\nfunction assertCompletionUsable(response, maxTokens, label) {\n  const choice = response \u0026\u0026 response.choices \u0026\u0026 response.choices[0];\n  if (!choice || !choice.message || typeof choice.message.content !== 'string') {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: model returned no content`);\n  }\n  if (choice.finish_reason === 'length') {\n    throw new Error(\n      `${label}: completion hit the ${maxTokens}-token ceiling (finish_reason=length). ` +\n        'Refusing to write a truncated page. Lower TRANSLATION_CHUNK_RATIO or raise TRANSLATION_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS.'\n    );\n  }\n  if (choice.finish_reason \u0026\u0026 !['stop', 'end_turn'].includes(choice.finish_reason)) {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: unexpected finish_reason \"${choice.finish_reason}\"`);\n  }\n  if (choice.message.content.trim() === '') {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: model returned an empty completion`);\n  }\n  return choice.message.content.trim();\n}\n\n/** One completion call, guarded. */\nasync function complete({ prompt, maxTokens, label }) {\n  const response = await getClient().chat.completions.create({\n    model: config.MODEL,\n    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],\n    temperature: config.TEMPERATURE,\n    max_tokens: maxTokens\n  });\n  return assertCompletionUsable(response, maxTokens, label);\n}\n\n/**\n * Chunk plan for a page body. Shared by dry-run and live runs.\n *\n * targetChars is the soft target: the source length whose estimated translation\n * lands on the chunk threshold. hardLimitChars is the source length whose\n * estimated translation would hit the completion ceiling. Overshooting the soft\n * target is fine (long code blocks contain no safe boundary); overshooting the\n * hard limit is a blocker, because that is where truncation would start.\n */\nfunction planChunks(body) {\n  const targetChars = Math.max(2000, Math.floor(config.chunkThresholdTokens() * 3.2 / 1.6));\n  const hardLimitChars = Math.floor((config.MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS * 3.2) / 1.6);\n  const { chunks, strategy } = splitMarkdown(body, targetChars);\n  const oversized = chunks\n    .map((chunk, idx) =\u003e (chunk.length \u003e hardLimitChars ? idx : -1))\n    .filter((idx) =\u003e idx !== -1);\n  return { chunks, strategy, oversized, targetChars, hardLimitChars };\n}\n\n/**\n * Refuse to translate a source page that is already structurally broken.\n * The parity check would reject the result anyway; failing here saves the spend\n * and points at the real problem, which is the English page.\n */\nfunction assertSourceWellFormed(body, filename) {\n  const scan = scanStructure(body.split('\\n'));\n  const problems = [];\n  if (scan.unbalancedFence) problems.push('unclosed code fence');\n  if (scan.unclosedJsx.length \u003e 0) problems.push(`unclosed JSX component(s): ${scan.unclosedJsx.join(', ')}`);\n  if (problems.length \u003e 0) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `${filename}: the ENGLISH source is structurally broken (${problems.join('; ')}). ` +\n        'Fix the English page first; translating it would produce a broken Chinese page.'\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nasync function translateMarkdown(content, filename) {\n  const gm = getMatter();\n  const { data: frontmatter, content: body } = gm(content);\n  assertFrontmatterUsable(frontmatter, `${filename} (source)`);\n  assertSourceWellFormed(body, filename);\n\n  const { chunks, strategy, oversized, hardLimitChars } = planChunks(body);\n\n  if (oversized.length \u003e 0) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `${filename}: ${oversized.length} chunk(s) exceed the ${hardLimitChars}-char safe limit ` +\n        `(strategy \"${strategy}\"). The page has a section with no safe boundary that is long enough to be ` +\n        'truncated. Split the page or raise TRANSLATION_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS. Refusing to emit a partial page.'\n    );\n  }\n\n  console.log(`  Translating body in ${chunks.length} chunk(s) (split strategy: ${strategy})`);\n\n  const translatedChunks = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i \u003c chunks.length; i++) {\n    const chunk = chunks[i];\n    const label = `${filename} chunk ${i + 1}/${chunks.length}`;\n    const budget = Math.min(\n      config.MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS,\n      Math.max(512, Math.ceil(config.estimateOutputTokens(chunk) * 1.3))\n    );\n    const raw = await complete({ prompt: buildBodyPrompt(chunk, i, chunks.length), maxTokens: budget, label });\n    translatedChunks.push(stripEchoedDelimiters(stripCodeBlockWrapper(raw), chunk));\n    if (i \u003c chunks.length - 1) await sleep(1000);\n  }\n\n  const translatedBody = translatedChunks.join('\\n\\n');\n\n  const problems = checkStructuralParity(body, translatedBody);\n  if (problems.length \u003e 0) {\n    throw new Error(`${filename}: translation failed integrity checks:\\n    - ${problems.join('\\n    - ')}`);\n  }\n\n  const translatedFrontmatter = {};\n  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(frontmatter)) {\n    if (typeof value === 'string' \u0026\u0026 !config.FRONTMATTER_PASSTHROUGH_KEYS.includes(key)) {\n      console.log(`  Translating frontmatter field: ${key}`);\n      const raw = await complete({\n        prompt: `Translate this to Simplified Chinese. Keep technical terms and brand names in English. Return only the translation: \"${value}\"`,\n        maxTokens: 300,\n        label: `${filename} frontmatter.${key}`\n      });\n      translatedFrontmatter[key] = raw.replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, '');\n    } else {\n      translatedFrontmatter[key] = value;\n    }\n  }\n\n  assertFrontmatterUsable(translatedFrontmatter, filename);\n\n  return gm.stringify(translatedBody, translatedFrontmatter);\n}\n\nfunction sleep(ms) {\n  return new Promise((resolve) =\u003e setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// File operations\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Translate one page and return the result in memory. Nothing is written here.\n *\n * Writing per page meant a run that failed on page 40 of 200 left 39 translated\n * pages and any deletions already applied in the working tree, while the closing\n * message still claimed no partial pages were written. CI never committed such a\n * batch, but a local run did leave one behind. The whole batch is now staged and\n * applied by `commitBatch` only once every page has passed.\n */\nasync function translateFile(sourcePath) {\n  const targetPath = config.getTargetPath(sourcePath, options.includeOptional);\n  if (!targetPath) {\n    console.log(`  Skipping ${sourcePath}: not in translation scope`);\n    return { status: 'skipped' };\n  }\n\n  const absSource = path.join(config.ROOT, sourcePath);\n\n  console.log(`\\nTranslating: ${sourcePath}`);\n  console.log(`  Target: ${targetPath}`);\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(absSource)) {\n    console.log('  Source file not found, skipping');\n    return { status: 'skipped' };\n  }\n\n  const content = fs.readFileSync(absSource, 'utf-8');\n  const translated = await translateMarkdown(content, sourcePath);\n\n  console.log(`  Staged ${targetPath}`);\n  return { status: 'translated', targetPath, content: translated };\n}\n\n/** Resolve the Chinese page whose English source has been deleted or renamed away. */\nfunction resolveOrphanedTarget(sourcePath) {\n  const targetPath = config.getTargetPath(sourcePath, options.includeOptional);\n  if (!targetPath) return null;\n  if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(config.ROOT, targetPath))) return null;\n  return targetPath;\n}\n\n/** Apply a fully translated batch to disk. Called only when nothing failed. */\nfunction commitBatch(writes, deletions) {\n  for (const { targetPath, content } of writes) {\n    const absTarget = path.join(config.ROOT, targetPath);\n    const targetDir = path.dirname(absTarget);\n    if (!fs.existsSync(targetDir)) {\n      fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });\n      console.log(`  Created directory: ${path.relative(config.ROOT, targetDir)}`);\n    }\n    fs.writeFileSync(absTarget, content, 'utf-8');\n    console.log(`  Wrote ${targetPath}`);\n  }\n  for (const targetPath of deletions) {\n    fs.unlinkSync(path.join(config.ROOT, targetPath));\n    console.log(`  Deleted ${targetPath} (English source removed)`);\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Dry run\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst INPUT_USD_PER_MTOK = Number(process.env.TRANSLATION_INPUT_USD_PER_MTOK || 2.5);\nconst OUTPUT_USD_PER_MTOK = Number(process.env.TRANSLATION_OUTPUT_USD_PER_MTOK || 10);\n\n/**\n * How many separate completion calls a page's frontmatter costs. `translateMarkdown`\n * sends one request per string value that is not on the passthrough list, so a page\n * with a title and a description is two calls on top of its body chunks.\n */\nfunction countTranslatableFrontmatterKeys(frontmatterText) {\n  if (!frontmatterText) return 0;\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const line of frontmatterText.split('\\n')) {\n    const match = line.match(/^([A-Za-z0-9_:.-]+)\\s*:\\s*(.*)$/);\n    if (!match) continue;\n    const [, key, value] = match;\n    if (config.FRONTMATTER_PASSTHROUGH_KEYS.includes(key)) continue;\n    if (value.trim() === '') continue;\n    count++;\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\nfunction dryRun(filesToTranslate, filesToDelete) {\n  console.log('\\nDRY RUN: no API calls, no files written, no API key required.');\n  console.log(`Model that would be used: ${config.MODEL}`);\n  console.log(`Completion ceiling: ${config.MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS} tokens, chunk above ${config.chunkThresholdTokens()} tokens`);\n  console.log(`Optional sections (cli, mcp-server, docs): ${options.includeOptional ? 'ENABLED' : 'disabled'}`);\n\n  let totalIn = 0;\n  let totalOut = 0;\n  let totalChunks = 0;\n  let totalFrontmatterCalls = 0;\n  const rows = [];\n  const blockers = [];\n\n  for (const file of filesToTranslate) {\n    const targetPath = config.getTargetPath(file, options.includeOptional);\n    const abs = path.join(config.ROOT, file);\n    if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) {\n      blockers.push(`${file}: source file missing`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const content = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf-8');\n    const { frontmatter, body } = config.splitFrontmatter(content);\n    const { chunks, strategy, oversized, hardLimitChars } = planChunks(body);\n\n    // Each translatable frontmatter value is its own completion call. Counting\n    // only body chunks understated a full pass by roughly 60 percent.\n    const frontmatterCalls = countTranslatableFrontmatterKeys(frontmatter);\n\n    const inTokens = config.estimateInputTokens(body);\n    const outTokens = config.estimateOutputTokens(body);\n    totalIn += inTokens + config.estimateInputTokens(frontmatter);\n    totalOut += outTokens + config.estimateOutputTokens(frontmatter);\n    totalChunks += chunks.length;\n    totalFrontmatterCalls += frontmatterCalls;\n\n    try {\n      assertSourceWellFormed(body, file);\n    } catch (error) {\n      blockers.push(error.message);\n    }\n\n    if (oversized.length \u003e 0) {\n      blockers.push(\n        `${file}: ${oversized.length} chunk(s) exceed the ${hardLimitChars}-char safe limit (strategy \"${strategy}\"); would FAIL rather than truncate`\n      );\n    }\n\n    rows.push({\n      file,\n      target: targetPath,\n      action: fs.existsSync(path.join(config.ROOT, targetPath)) ? 'update' : 'create',\n      bytes: content.length,\n      inTokens,\n      outTokens,\n      chunks: chunks.length,\n      strategy: chunks.length \u003e 1 ? strategy : '-',\n      wouldTruncateBefore: outTokens \u003e 4000\n    });\n  }\n\n  console.log(`\\nPlanned translations: ${rows.length}`);\n  if (rows.length \u003e 0) {\n    const w = (s, n) =\u003e String(s).padEnd(n).slice(0, n);\n    console.log(\n      `  ${w('ACTION', 7)}${w('CHUNKS', 7)}${w('IN_TOK', 8)}${w('OUT_TOK', 8)}${w('OLD_4K_TRUNC', 14)}SOURCE -\u003e TARGET`\n    );\n    for (const r of rows) {\n      console.log(\n        `  ${w(r.action, 7)}${w(r.chunks, 7)}${w(r.inTokens, 8)}${w(r.outTokens, 8)}${w(r.wouldTruncateBefore ? No files were written.');\n  return 0;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function main() {\n  console.log('LI.FI Documentation Translation System');\n  console.log('='.repeat(60));\n\n  const sections = config.classifySections();\n  if (sections.unclassified.length \u003e 0) {\n    console.error(\n      `\\nERROR: unclassified documentation section(s): ${sections.unclassified.join(', ')}\\n` +\n        'Add each one to REQUIRED_SECTIONS, OPTIONAL_SECTIONS or EXCLUDED_PATHS in scripts/translation-config.js.\\n' +\n        'Refusing to run so new sections cannot silently fall out of translation scope.'\n    );\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  if (!options.dryRun \u0026\u0026 !process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY) {\n    console.error('ERROR: OPENAI_API_KEY is not set. process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"54:T59f,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"const fs = require(\"fs\");\nconst path = require(\"path\");\n\nconst docsPath Long pages came back clipped and the clipped file was\n * written to disk. This module splits a page at safe boundaries so every request\n * stays inside the completion budget, and provides parity checks so a damaged\n * translation fails loudly instead of being committed.\n *\n * A boundary is only \"safe\" when it is:\n *   - outside a fenced code block, and\n *   - outside any open JSX/Mintlify component (\u003cSteps\u003e, \u003cCardGroup\u003e, \u003cTabs\u003e, ...)\n *\n * No network access, no dependencies. Values are returned verbatim so the parity check can compare\n * them byte for byte.\n */\nfunction parseAttributes(raw) {\n  const attrs = [];\n  if (!raw) return attrs;\n  let i = 0;\n  while (i \u003c raw.length) {\n    while (i \u003c raw.length \u0026\u0026 /\\s/.test(raw[i])) i++;\n    if (i \u003e= raw.length) break;\n\n    const nameStart = i;\n    while (i \u003c raw.length \u0026\u0026 /[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]/.test(raw[i])) i++;\n    const name = raw.slice(nameStart, i);\n    if (!name) {\n      i++;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const afterName = i;\n    while (i \u003c raw.length \u0026\u0026 /\\s/.test(raw[i])) i++;\n    if (raw[i] !== '=') {\n      attrs.push({ name, value: null });\n      i = afterName;\n      continue;\n    }\n    i++;\n    while (i \u003c raw.length \u0026\u0026 /\\s/.test(raw[i])) i++;\n\n    const opener = raw[i];\n    if (opener === '\"' || opener === \"'\") {\n      const end = raw.indexOf(opener, i + 1);\n      if (end === -1) {\n        attrs.push({ name, value: raw.slice(i + 1) });\n        break;\n      }\n      attrs.push({ name, value: raw.slice(i + 1, end) });\n      i = end + 1;\n    } else if (opener === '{') {\n      let depth = 0;\n      let j = i;\n      for (; j \u003c raw.length; j++) {\n        if (raw[j] === '{') depth++;\n        else if (raw[j] === '}') {\n          depth--;\n          if (depth === 0) {\n            j++;\n            break;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      attrs.push({ name, value: raw.slice(i, j) });\n      i = j;\n    } else {\n      const valueStart = i;\n      while (i \u003c raw.length \u0026\u0026 !/\\s/.test(raw[i])) i++;\n      attrs.push({ name, value: raw.slice(valueStart, i) });\n    }\n  }\n  return attrs;\n}\n\n/**\n * Walk the document line by line, recording the fence and JSX-nesting state that\n * is in effect *before* each line. jsxStack = [];\n\n  const applyTag = (raw, lineIndex) =\u003e {\n    const tag = parseTag(raw);\n    if (!tag) return;\n    tags.push({ ...tag, lineIndex });\n    if (tag.closing) {\n      const idx = jsxStack.lastIndexOf(tag.name);\n      if (idx !== -1) jsxStack.splice(idx, 1);\n    } else if (!tag.selfClosing) {\n      jsxStack.push(tag.name);\n    }\n  };\n\n  lines.forEach((line, lineIndex) */\nfunction stripFencedBlocks(text) {\n  const out = [];\n  let open = null;\n  for (const line of text.split('\\n')) {\n    const fenceMatch = line.match(FENCE_RE);\n    if (fenceMatch) {\n Files under these prefixes are translation output, never input. */\nconst TARGET_PREFIXES = ['zh-Hans/', 'zh-hans-api-reference/'];\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Model / request configuration\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst MODEL = process.env.TRANSLATION_MODEL || 'gpt-4o';\nconst FRONTMATTER_MODEL = process.env.TRANSLATION_FRONTMATTER_MODEL || MODEL;\nconst TEMPERATURE = Number(process.env.TRANSLATION_TEMPERATURE || 0.3);\n\n/**\n * Hard ceiling on completion tokens per request. gpt-4o allows 16384; we leave\n * headroom and chunk anything that would come close, rather than relying on the\n * ceiling to clip output.\n */\nconst MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = Number(process.env.TRANSLATION_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS || 8000);\n\n/**\n * Chunk when the estimated completion size exceeds this fraction of the ceiling.\n * Chinese output is token-dense relative to English input, so stay well under.\n */\nconst CHUNK_THRESHOLD_RATIO = Number(process.env.TRANSLATION_CHUNK_RATIO || 0.55);\n\n/** Rough English-input chars per token. Deliberately low (pessimistic). */\nconst CHARS_PER_INPUT_TOKEN = 3.2;\n\n/** Assumed output-token growth factor for EN -\u003e zh-Hans. Deliberately high. */\nconst OUTPUT_TOKEN_GROWTH = 1.6;\n\n/** Frontmatter values never get translated. */\n// Frontmatter values that are routing, configuration or asset references rather\n// than prose. `slug` belongs here: 15 English pages set it, and translating it\n// renames the published Chinese route, breaking every inbound link to that page\n// and the docs.json entry that points at it.\nconst FRONTMATTER_PASSTHROUGH_KEYS = [\n  'icon',\n  'openapi',\n  'api',\n  'url',\n  'slug',\n  'mode',\n  'version',\n  'authMethod',\n  'og:image',\n  'og:url',\n  'twitter:image'\n];\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction includeOptionalFromEnv(argv = process.argv) {\n  if (argv.includes('--include-optional')) return true;\n  const env = (process.env.TRANSLATE_INCLUDE_OPTIONAL || '').toLowerCase();\n  return env === 'true' || env === '1' || env === 'yes';\n}\n\n/** Build the active mapping table. */\nfunction getPathMappings(includeOptional = false) {\n  return includeOptional\n    ? sourcePath === enPath : sourcePath.startsWith(enPath)) {\n      return sourcePath.replace(enPath, zhPath);\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Reverse-map a Chinese target path back to its English source path. */\nfunction getSourcePath(targetPath, includeOptional = true) {\n  const mappings = getPathMappings(includeOptional);\n  // Longest target prefix wins so zh-Hans/ does not shadow a nested mapping.\n  const entries = Object.entries(mappings).sort((a, b) =\u003e b[1].length - a[1].length);\n  for (const [enPath, zhPath] of entries) {\n    const isFileMapping = !zhPath.endsWith('/');\n    if (isFileMapping ? targetPath === zhPath : targetPath.startsWith(zhPath)) {\n      return targetPath.replace(zhPath, enPath);\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** True when a changed file is eligible to be translated. */\nfunction isTranslatable(filePath, includeOptional = false) {\n  if (!filePath.endsWith('.mdx')) return false;\n  if (isTargetPath(filePath)) return false;\n  if (isExcluded(filePath)) return false;\n  return getTargetPath(filePath, includeOptional) !== null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Repo scanning\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst WALK_SKIP_DIRS = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', '.mintlify', '.playwright-mcp', 'images', 'logo']);\n\nfunction walkMdx(dir = ROOT, files = []) {\n  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n      if (!WALK_SKIP_DIRS.has(entry.name)) walkMdx(path.join(dir, entry.name), files);\n    } else if (entry.name.endsWith('.mdx')) {\n      files.push(toRepoRelative(path.join(dir, entry.name)));\n    }\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\n/** All English source pages currently in the repo, sorted. */\nfunction listEnglishPages() {\n  return walkMdx()\n    .filter((f) =\u003e !isTargetPath(f))\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/** All Chinese pages currently in the repo, sorted. */\nfunction listChinesePages() {\n  return walkMdx()\n    .filter((f) =\u003e isTargetPath(f))\n    .sort();\n}\n\n/**\n * Classify every top-level section that holds .mdx pages.\n * `unclassified` being non-empty is a configuration bug.\n */\nfunction classifySections() {\n  const sections = new Set();\n  for (const file of listEnglishPages()) {\n    sections.add(file.includes('/') ? `${file.split('/')[0]}/` : file);\n  }\n\n  const result = { required: [], optional: [], excluded: [], unclassified: [] };\n  for (const section of [...sections].sort()) {\n    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(REQUIRED_SECTIONS, section)) result.required.push(section);\n    else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(OPTIONAL_SECTIONS, section)) result.optional.push(section);\n    else if (isExcluded(section)) result.excluded.push(section);\n    else result.unclassified.push(section);\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\n/** Every mapped English page, i.e. the work list for a full catch-up pass. */\nfunction listTranslatablePages(includeOptional = false) {\n  return listEnglishPages().filter((f) =\u003e isTranslatable(f, includeOptional));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Frontmatter\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Minimal frontmatter splitter.\n *\n * Deliberately dependency-free so that --dry-run and the coverage verifier work\n * on a fresh clone with no node_modules. for (const section of Object.keys(config.OPTIONAL_SECTIONS)) {\n      const probe = section.endsWith('/') ? `${section}probe.mdx` : section;\n      if (config.getTargetPath(probe, false) !== null) {\n        throw new Error(`${section} is reachable with optional sections disabled`);\n      }\n      if (config.getTargetPath(probe, true) === null) {\n        throw new Error(`${section} is unreachable ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Rendering\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction renderMarkdown(data) {\n  const lines = [];\n  const push = (s = '') =\u003e lines.push(s);\n\n  push('---');\n  push('title: Chinese Docs Translation - Drift Report');\n  push('type: generated-report');\n  push(`generated: ${data.generatedAt}`);\n  push('generator: scripts/verify-translation-coverage.js --markdown');\n  push('repo: lifinance/public-docs');\n  push('---');\n sections (cli, mcp-server, docs): ${data.includeOptional ? 'included' : 'excluded'}.`);\n  push();\n  push('## Summary');\n  push();\n  push('| Metric | Count |');\n  push('| --- | --- |');\n  push(`| English pages in repo | ${data.counts.englishPages} |`);\n  push(`| Chinese pages in repo | ${data.counts.chinesePages} |`);\n  push(`| English pages in translation scope | ${data.counts.inScope} |`);\n  push(`| No Chinese counterpart | ${data.counts.missing} |`);\n  push(`| Chinese counterpart stale vs English | ${data.counts.stale} |`);\n  push(`| Chinese counterpart up to date | ${data.counts.current} |`);\n  push(`| Orphaned Chinese pages | ${data.counts.orphans} |`);\n  push();\n  push('## Section classification');\n  push();\n  push(`- Translated: ${data.sections.required.join(', ') || 'none'}`);\n  push(`- Optional, currently off: ${data.sections.optional.join(', ') || 'none'}`);\n  push(`- Excluded by design: ${data.sections.excluded.join(', ') || 'none'}`);\n  push(`- Unclassified (configuration error): ${data.sections.unclassified.join(', ') || 'none'}`);\n  push();\n  push('## Coverage by section');\n  push();\n  push('| Section | In scope | Missing | Stale | Current |');\n  push('| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |');\n  for (const [section, s] of Object.entries(data.bySection).sort()) {\n    push(`| ${section} | ${s.total} | ${s.missing} | ${s.stale} | ${s.current} |`);\n  }\n  push();\n  push(`## English pages with no Chinese counterpart (${data.missing.length})`);\n  push();\n  if (data.missing.length === 0) push('None.');\n  else {\n    push('| English page | Would create | English last changed |');\n    push('| --- | --- | --- |');\n    data.missing.forEach((m) =\u003e\n      push(`| ${m.source} | ${m.target} | ${m.sourceDate ? m.sourceDate.slice(0, 10) : 'untracked'} |`)\n    );\n  }\n  push();\n  push(`## Stale Chinese pages (${data.stale.length})`);\n  push();\n  if (data.stale.length === 0) push('None.');\n  else {\n    push('| English page | Chinese page | English last changed | Chinese last changed | Days behind |');\n    push('| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |');\n    data.stale\n      .slice()\n      .sort((a, b) =\u003e b.daysBehind - a.daysBehind)\n      .forEach((s) =\u003e\n        push(\n          `| ${s.source} | ${s.target} | ${s.sourceDate.slice(0, 10)} | ${s.targetDate.slice(0, 10)} | ${s.daysBehind} |`\n        )\n      );\n  }\n  push();\n  push(`## Orphaned Chinese pages (${data.orphans.length})`);\n  push();\n  if (data.orphans.length === 0) push('None.');\n  else {\n    push('| Chinese page | Reason |');\n    push('| --- | --- |');\n    data.orphans.forEach((o) =\u003e push(`| ${o.target} | ${o.reason} |`));\n  }\n  push();\n  push('## Navigation parity (docs.json)');\n  push();\n  push('| Language | Tabs | Navigation entries |');\n  push('| --- | --- | --- |');\n  if (data.nav.en) push(`| en | ${data.nav.en.tabs} | ${data.nav.en.pages} |`);\n  if (data.nav.zh) push(`| zh-Hans | ${data.nav.zh.tabs} | ${data.nav.zh.pages} |`);\n  push();\n  push(\n    `Chinese pages on disk that are not reachable from the zh-Hans navigation: ${data.nav.zhFilesNotInNav.length}`\n  );\n  data.nav.zhFilesNotInNav.forEach((p) =\u003e push(`- ${p}`));\n  push();\n  push(`zh-Hans navigation entries with no file on disk: ${data.nav.zhNavEntriesWithoutFile.length}`);\n  data.nav.zhNavEntriesWithoutFile.forEach((p) =\u003e push(`- ${p}`));\n  push();\n  push('## Note on navigation');\n  push();\n  push(\n    'The translation script writes .mdx files but does not edit docs.json. 'ENABLED' : 'disabled'}`);\n  lines.push(`Model configured:  ${config.MODEL}`);\n  lines.push('');\n  lines.push('Section classification');\n  lines.push(`  translated: pages:    ${data.counts.orphans}`);\n  lines.push('');\n  lines.push('Per section (in-scope / missing / stale / current)');\n  for (const [section, s] of Object.entries(data.bySection).sort()) {\n    lines.push(`  ${section.padEnd(18)} ${String(s.total).padStart(3)} / ${String(s.missing).padStart(3)} / ${String(s.stale).padStart(3)} / ${String(s.current).padStart(3)}`);\n  }\n  lines.push('');\n  lines.push('docs.json navigation');\n  if (data.nav.en) lines.push(`  en:      ${data.nav.en.tabs} tabs, ${data.nav.en.pages} entries`);\n  if (data.nav.zh) lines.push(`  zh-Hans: ${data.nav.zh.tabs} ${data.nav.zhNavEntriesWithoutFile.length}`);\n  return lines.join('\\n');\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction main() {\n  if (SELF_TEST) {\n    const { checks, failures, brokenSources } = selfTest();\n renderSummary(data);\n\n  if (OUT_FILE) {\n    fs.writeFileSync(OUT_FILE, output.endsWith('\\n') ? output : `${output}\\n`, 'utf-8');\n    console.log(`Wrote ${OUT_FILE}`);\n  } else {\n
